Architecture migration — implementation notes

Initiative: full active src/ alignment — pause is reference impl; scheduling is pause-first per tier
Branch: cursor/arch-migrate-src-ca70
Spec: pause-client-architecture.md, holzen-application-architecture.md
Workflow: implementation-notes-workflow.md

Contents

  • Agreed baseline (2026-05)
  • Running notes

Agreed baseline (2026-05)

Captured when pause-client-architecture.md was accepted — not deviations; the interpretation we implement toward.

  • Ritual layout: ritual/reflect/ and ritual/frame/ — not peer top-level companion/ + reflect/ (organize by beat, not “AI”).
  • Companions: inside features/pause; /companion/* routes are pause presentation.
  • UI: JSX in screens/pause/components/; hooks in features/pause/presentation/.
  • Collections: user Archive Collections tab not MVP — strip to legacy (spec §7); keep companion_catalog and slot favorites.
  • Branch: one long-lived migration branch; one commit per vertical slice.
  • Scope vs priority: north star = all bounded contexts on the §9 skeleton; scheduling = pause-first at each slice tier, then subscription/auth/market/… (see README.md phases).
  • Other contexts: same module skeleton as pause (spec §9) — not a separate initiative.

Running notes

2026-05-20 Spec §3, §10 (transitional) open

Legacy use-case folder names still on disk

Design decision
Incremental migration: new work uses ritual/reflect/ and ritual/frame/; existing application/use-cases/companion/ and application/use-cases/reflect/ are moved slice-by-slice rather than a big-bang rename.
Deviation
Tree on disk temporarily does not match spec §3 literally — spec §10 documents this as transitional.
Tradeoffs
Big-bang rename — cleaner tree, huge diff, hard review. Incremental — slower convergence, safer merges. Picked incremental.
Open questions
Should we add a CI warning when new files land in old top-level companion/ or reflect/ folders (not under ritual/)?
2026-05-20 Spec §7 resolved

Collections strip — resolved (2026-05-20)

Resolution
Stripped Archive Collections tab; moved usePauseLibraryBundle + UI to src/legacy/archive-collections/; check:active-surface blocks .from('collections'|'collection_items') in active src/; ?seg=collections redirects to Pauses.
Design decision (historical)
Document Collections as non-MVP in active-surface.md and IA before removing Archive tab code — spec-first, then strip slice.
Tradeoffs
Strip now — smaller active surface sooner. Spec then strip — reviewers agree on scope before deletion. Picked spec then strip.
Open questions
After UI strip: do collections / collection_items tables move to Tier B in active-surface.md, or stay in DB unused? Decision: tables stay in DB (delete-account, RLS); client queries quarantined — not Tier B.
2026-05-20 Spec §6 (public.ts) open

public.ts still larger than slim target

Design decision
Continue exporting from public.ts for backward compatibility while migrating call sites; peel to public-queries / public-rpc per slice.
Deviations
None intentional — convergence in progress.
Tradeoffs
Hard export cap in CI — forces splits quickly but noisy. Soft target in spec — flexible during migration. Picked soft target until migration completes.
Open questions
Add check:public-barrel-size when public.ts stabilizes?
2026-05-20 Initiative scope resolved

Pause-first scheduling ≠ pause-only initiative

Design decision
North star: align all active src/ and extend the pause module template (spec §9) to subscription, auth, market, user, notifications. Scheduling: at each slice tier, finish pause violations before the same tier in another context — pause is reference impl, not the sole domain.
Deviation
None — clarifies wording that was easy to read as “only touch pause until the branch merges.”
Tradeoffs
Pause-only until done — fastest ritual convergence, delays cross-context debt. Strict tier rotation (pause tier 1, then auth tier 1, …) — spreads patterns early, fragments pause ritual moves. Picked pause-first per tier with explicit Phase B/C in migration README.
Open questions
Before Phase B slices: add per-context client-architecture docs or rely on AGENTS + feature READMEs? Decision: add scaffold *-client-architecture.md for each shipped context + client-architecture-overview.md for full src/ rings (Phase 0) before Phase B code slices.
